**Table 7:** Controlled 3-seed Native `50,332,176` long-context ($T=2,048$, 100 steps $= 13.1\text{M}$ tokens) results across Delayed Installation (`exp29`), True Both-Stages Pre-Install (`exp30`), Strict Post-Installation Construction (`exp32`), Fused Triton Strict Post-Install (`exp40`, `exp41`), Native `50.3M` `SR-LNP K=16` (`exp41`), our $2 \times 2$ `{M_g = 8, M_g = 1} x {lr_embed = 3e-3, lr_embed = 0.015}` GPU-Resident Fused-Triton factorial (`exp43_native50m_master_results.json`), our `scale_factor = 8.0` and $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ Pinned-Ring-Buffer controls (`exp44_native50m_controls_results.json`, `9.61–9.69 GiB` peak VRAM), our 3-seed 5-matrix Spherical Active-$W_Q$ transfer (`exp46_native50m_active_q_results.json`, `9.80 GiB` peak VRAM across `48.23M` active parameters), our 3-seed Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` Decoupled Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ transfer at $M=2$ and $M=4$ (`exp55_native50m_upper_half_qk_results.json`, `10.09 GiB` peak VRAM across `48.23M` active parameters), our 3-seed full 6-matrix (`50,331,648 / 50,332,176 = 99.999%` active parameters) Decoupled Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ transfer at $M=1$ and $M=2$ (`exp48_native50m_6mat_active_qk_results.json`, `10.08–10.24 GiB` peak VRAM), and our 3-seed Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` closed-form `Wiener-MMSE` ($M=2$) and `Wiener-MMSE + Hadamard-4` ($M=4$) transfer (`exp61_native50m_wiener_mmse_key_gather_results.json`, `9.96–10.06 GiB` peak VRAM across `48.23M` active parameters). Note: all timing columns report 3-seed means; in `exp29`–`exp32`, the third timing column reports `t_wall_step_ms_including_eval_mean`, whereas in `exp40`–`exp48`, `exp55`, and `exp61` it reports `Fwd + Rule` (`t_total_step_gpu_ms`); matched `Fwd + Rule` comparisons use `1,425.01 + 6,432.04 = 7,857.05 ms` for `exp32`.

**Genuinely Isolated Kernel Substitution (`benchmark_native50m_isolated_kernel.json`) vs. `exp43` Implementation-Package Speedup, $2 \times 2$ `{M_g, lr_embed}` Factorial, `exp44` Controls, and `exp46`/`exp48`/`exp55`/`exp61` Native 5/6-Matrix & Upper-Half Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ Scale-Transfer:**
1. **Separating the `exp43` Combined Implementation Package from Genuinely Isolated Kernel Substitutions:**
   In `exp40` (`1.9845 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB at `lr_wte = 0.10`) and `exp41` (`2.0252 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB at `lr_wte = 0.015`), we had changed the `wte` credit path and optimizer schedule (replacing `exp32`'s Stage-3 `lm_head` lexical contraction on `dfwd_x_final_wte` with the diagonal skip surrogate and applying `lrm -> 0.2, eps = 1e-4` to `lm_head`). In `exp43` (`native50m_strict_post_install_M8_exp32_exact_triton`), we preserve `exp32`'s exact Stage-3 lexical readout and optimizer schedule (`eps = 1e-8`, constant AdamW LR, `lrm: 1.0 -> 0.5` on Muon), scoring **`1.9359 / 1.9356 / 1.9360` $\to$ `1.9358 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB (`1.9205 ± 0.0002` VAL-B BPB, `231.97` PPL)** and matching `exp32` (`1.9361 ± 0.0004`) to within `0.0003` BPB.
   However, `run_exp43_native50m_mg1.py` differs from `exp32_strict_post_install_construction.py` in several systems and precision components beyond the WM contraction kernel: `exp32` passes `FP32` `q_r, k_r, v` to SDPA (`fa3.flash_attn_func`), computes `FP32` attention-filtered traces during the forward pass, offloads delayed traces to unpinned CPU memory, orthogonalizes hidden-matrix updates individually, and uses a dense one-hot target at `lm_head` (**`9.11 GiB` peak VRAM**, **`1,425.01 + 6,432.04 = 7,857.05 ms/step` on matched `fwd + rule` timed regions**, and `8,070.22 ms` `t_wall_step_ms_including_eval_mean`), whereas `exp43` casts `q_r, k_r, v` to `BF16` before SDPA (`run_exp43_native50m_mg1.py:243-248`), keeps delayed traces on GPU with pinned-memory ring buffers only for the 4D MLP activations (`pin_h_mlp, pin_dfwd_h`), reconstructs Stage-1 attention-filtered inputs at consumption time from `BF16` cached `q_rs, k_rs, norm_attn_ins` (`run_exp43_native50m_mg1.py:159-161`), batches `Pre-Polar Muon` orthogonalization across layers, uses an in-place scatter at `lm_head`, and replaces `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast` with fused Triton kernels (**`20.57 GiB` peak VRAM**, **`1,332.65 + 1,659.19 = 2,991.84 ms/step` on matched `fwd + rule` timed regions**). Thus, comparing `exp32` to `exp43` measures a **combined implementation package** that achieves a **`2.63x` speedup on matched `fwd + rule` timed regions (`7,857.05 -> 2,991.84 ms/step`, and `3.88x` on the learning-rule region `6,432.04 -> 1,659.19 ms`)** in exchange for **`+11.46 GiB` higher peak GPU memory (`9.11 -> 20.57 GiB`)**—or **`9.61–9.69 GiB` peak VRAM (`4,872.5` and `5,371.7 ms/step` 3-seed mean `fwd + rule` in Table 7)** when all 8 delayed trace tensors (`pin_h_mlp, pin_y_attn, pin_s_lm, pin_dfwd_h, pin_norm_mlp, pin_norm_attn, pin_q_r, pin_k_r`) are placed in pinned host ring buffers (`run_exp44_native50m_controls.py`).
   To establish the **genuinely isolated effect of the fused Triton contraction kernels** with all storage, precision, trace construction, optimizer execution, head calculation, and timing boundaries held 100% identical, we execute three isolated benchmarks in `benchmark_native50m_isolated_kernel.py` (`benchmark_native50m_isolated_kernel.json`, where the 3 steady-state samples measure within-run steady-state variation):
   - **Standalone 288-Call/Step Kernel Microbenchmark (`Part 1`, `2` warmup + `10` timed iterations, or `4` timed iterations for $M_g=1$ PyTorch):** `exp32`'s PyTorch baseline (`compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast`) processes stream chunks (`chunk_G = 256` by default, allocating **`4,618.5–4,632.5 MB`** of temporary 3D chunk workspace; or `chunk_G = 32`, allocating **`618.1–632.1 MB`**). Across the 18 WM contractions per microbatch (`288` calls/step), replacing `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast` with `compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast` (`0.0 MB` 3D workspace; only the `8.0 MB` or `64.0 MB` output buffer) speeds up the 288-call suite **`8.35x` vs. `chunk_G=256` (`2,749.29 ± 5.56 -> 329.41 ± 0.90 ms/step`) and `7.15x` vs. `chunk_G=32` (`2,353.90 ± 1.65 -> 329.41 ± 0.90 ms/step`)** at $M_g=8$ (`lm_head_cos = 0.99999733, rel_l2 = 2.34e-3`), and **`36.74x` vs. `chunk_G=256` (`21,437.38 ± 14.37 -> 583.45 ± 0.89 ms/step`) and `31.31x` vs. `chunk_G=32` (`18,270.57 ± 12.66 -> 583.45 ± 0.89 ms/step`)** at $M_g=1$ (`lm_head_cos = 0.99999994, rel_l2 = 1.52e-7`).
   - **Isolated Substitution Inside `exp32`'s Exact CPU-Offloaded Trainer (`Part 2`, $M_g=8$, `3`-step CPU-heap warmup followed by `5` steps `0–4` including pipeline startup `steps 0–1` and `3` steady-state steps `2–4`):** Swapping *only* `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast(chunk_G=256)` $\to$ `compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast` inside `exp32_strict_post_install_construction.py`'s exact CPU-offloaded trainer reduces **steady-state (`steps 2–4`, where all 288 calls/step are active) learning-rule time `1.60x` (`6,456.78 ± 0.64 -> 4,039.89 ± 0.20 ms/step`, saving `2,416.89 ms/step`—matching Part 1's `2,419.88 ms/step` kernel savings to within `3.0 ms`) and steady-state `fwd + rule` step time `1.44x` (`7,876.97 ± 0.65 -> 5,460.10 ± 0.52 ms/step`)**; averaged across all `5` steps `0–4` including pipeline startup (`step 0: 1,841.2 vs. 1,840.7 ms` rule; `step 1: 3,764.9 vs. 2,683.6 ms` rule), it reduces rule time **`1.50x` (`4,995.29 ± 2,113.64 -> 3,328.78 ± 1,018.30 ms/step`)** and `fwd + rule` time **`1.35x` (`6,415.55 ± 2,113.31 -> 4,749.72 ± 1,016.99 ms/step`)**, while **reducing peak VRAM from `9.108 GiB` to `7.629 GiB` (`-1.479 GiB`)** and matching step-5 DEV BPB identically (`2.6686` vs. `2.6686`).
   - **Isolated Substitution Inside `exp43`'s Exact GPU-Resident Trainer (`Part 3`, $M_g=8$ and $M_g=1$, `2` warmup steps `0–1` + `3` timed steady-state steps `2–4` with per-contraction `cuda.synchronize()` around `contract_fn`):** Inside `run_exp43_native50m_mg1.py`'s GPU-resident trainer (`19.50 GiB` base allocation), `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast` with `chunk_G=256` (`4.62 GB` temp HBM) exceeds the `23.55 GiB` capacity of an RTX 4090 (`OOM`), requiring `chunk_G=32` (`~618 MB` temp HBM) to fit on GPU. Holding `exp43`'s GPU-resident storage, pinned MLP ring buffers, `BF16` SDPA and deferred Stage-1 attention-filtered input reconstruction, batched Muon orthogonalization, in-place scatter head calculation, and timing boundaries 100% identical (`20.566 GiB` peak VRAM at $M_g=8$; `20.646 GiB` at $M_g=1$), swapping *only* `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast(chunk_G=32)` $\to$ `compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast` speeds up the 288-call contraction **`6.86x` (`2,363.30 ± 0.72 -> 344.60 ± 0.74 ms`)**, the learning rule **`2.19x` (`3,725.38 ± 0.69 -> 1,702.87 ± 0.98 ms`)**, and `fwd + rule` step time **`1.67x` (`5,055.97 ± 1.30 -> 3,033.91 ± 1.36 ms`)** at $M_g=8$, and speeds up the 288-call contraction **`29.54x` (`18,317.09 ± 20.32 -> 620.01 ± 0.22 ms`)**, the learning rule **`9.91x` (`19,689.83 ± 20.99 -> 1,987.43 ± 0.59 ms`)**, and `fwd + rule` step time **`6.33x` (`21,022.47 ± 21.62 -> 3,319.91 ± 0.59 ms`)** at $M_g=1$.
2. **Complete $2 \times 2$ `{M_g = 8, M_g = 1} x {lr_embed = 3e-3, lr_embed = 0.015}` Factorial (`exp43`) and `scale_factor = 8.0` & $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ Controls (`exp44`):**
   - **Isolating $M_g: 8 \to 1$ Cross-Token Interference vs. $1/M_g$ Normalization on Native `50.3M` (`exp43` + `exp44`):** At `lr_embed = 3e-3`, reducing $M_g: 8 \to 1$ ($G_{\text{micro}}: 1,024 \to 8,192$) under `scale_factor = 1.0` improves 100-step Native `50.3M` Strict Post-Install DEV BPB by **`-0.0256` BPB (`1.9358 ± 0.0002 -> 1.9102 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB, `1.8966 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `215.84` PPL)** in `3,271.7 ms/step` (`fwd + rule`). In `exp44` (`native50m_strict_post_install_M8_scaleMg8_triton`), setting `scale_factor = M_g = 8.0` at $M_g = 8$ (so the $1/M_g$ divisor in `compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast` is removed, matching $M_g=1$'s unit prefactor `mult = 1.0`) yields **`1.9350 / 1.9347 / 1.9353` $\to$ `1.9350 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB (`1.9197 ± 0.0003` VAL-B, `231.42` PPL)**—differing from `scale_factor = 1.0` (`1.9358 ± 0.0002`) by only **`-0.0008` BPB** because `Pre-Polar Muon` and `AdamW` are nearly scale-invariant; as a descriptive decomposition along this tested intervention path ($M_g = 8, \text{scale\_factor} = 1.0 \to M_g = 8, \text{scale\_factor} = 8.0 \to M_g = 1, \text{scale\_factor} = 1.0$), eliminating the $M_g - 1 = 7$ within-stream cross-token interference terms accounts for **`96.9%` (`-0.0248` of `-0.0256` BPB, `1.9350 -> 1.9102`)** of the net $M_g: 8 \to 1$ improvement.
   - **Scale-Matched Embedding Velocity (`lr_embed = 0.015`) vs. Stage-3 $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ Damping at $BT = 131,072$ (`exp43` + `exp44`):** Increasing `lr_embed` to `0.015` ($= 0.12 / \sqrt{64}$) under `exp32`'s exact `dfwd_x_final_wte` lexical contraction and $\epsilon = 10^{-8}$ improves $M_g = 8$ by **`-0.0198` BPB (`1.9358 -> 1.9160 ± 0.0003`)** in `2,988.6 ms/step` and improves $M_g = 1$ to **`1.8978 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.8844 ± 0.0003` VAL-B BPB, `208.39` PPL)** in `3,263.7 ms/step` (`-0.0383` BPB ahead of `exp32`). Conversely, in `exp44` (`native50m_strict_post_install_M1_wte15e3_eps1e4_triton`), changing *only* Stage-3 `lm_head` AdamW `eps` from $10^{-8} \to 10^{-4}$ at $M_g = 1, \text{lr\_embed} = 0.015$ (while preserving `dfwd_x_final_wte` and `lrm: 1.0 -> 0.5`) degrades 100-step Native `50.3M` DEV BPB by **`+0.0288` BPB (`1.8978 ± 0.0007 -> 1.9266 ± 0.0004` DEV BPB, `1.9117 ± 0.0001` VAL-B, `226.01` PPL)**. This empirical contrast is consistent with the batch-size scaling mechanism of Proposition 4(2) and Section 4.3 (`exp36` Part 0A): on `d6` ($BT = 2,048$, `360` steps), single-step target-token collisions are frequent and $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ damps post-installation drift (`-0.0279` BPB), whereas on Native `50.3M` ($BT = 131,072 = 64\times$ larger batch, `100` steps), $1/BT$ already dilutes single-token self-update collisions $64\times$ and $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ over-damps rare-token `lm_head` adaptation over a 100-step horizon.
3. **Scale-Transfer of 5-Matrix Spherical Active-$W_Q$ (`exp46`), Selective Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ at $M=2$ and $M=4$ under $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`exp55`) and Closed-Form `Wiener-MMSE` + `Hadamard-4` (`exp61`), and Full 8-Block 6-Matrix (`99.9990%` Active Parameters) Decoupled Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ at $M=1$ and $M=2$ (`exp48`) to Long-Context Native `50,332,176`:**
   - **5-Matrix Spherical Active-$W_Q$ (`exp46`):** Transferring the Spherical Active-$W_Q$ rule to Native `50.3M` ($L=8, D=512, H=4, d_h=128, T=2,048$, $M_g=1$, `48,234,496 / 50,332,176 = 95.83%` active parameters, $\eta_Q = 0.048, \beta_Q = 0.95$) under strict post-install delayed construction with full pinned host ring buffers, deterministic on-the-fly `xi_q` regeneration, and `BF16`-approximate inverse-RoPE recovery of $q_n$ (`q_n_p2 = apply_rotary_emb(q_r_p2.float(), cos_full, -sin_full)` from `BF16` cached `q_r_p2`) achieves **`1.8889 / 1.8897 / 1.8905` $\to$ `1.8897 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB (`1.8765 ± 0.0013` VAL-B BPB, `203.72` PPL)** across Seeds `46, 47, 48` in a 3-seed mean of **`6,012.9 ms/step` (`2,195.2 ms` fwd + `3,817.7 ms` rule)** and **`9.80 GiB` peak VRAM**.
   - **Full 8-Block 6-Matrix (`99.9990%` Active Parameters) Decoupled Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ at $M=1$ and $M=2$ (`exp48`):** Extending Native `50.3M` to all 6 principal hidden matrix families (`50,331,648 / 50,332,176 = 99.9990%` of total parameters active; the `528` frozen parameters consist of `512` `ve_gate` matrix weights across the 4 value-embedding layers plus `16` `resid_lambdas, x0_lambdas` scalars) with decoupled query/key forward displacement probes constructed during the originating forward pass ($t$, performing $1$ clean attention evaluation plus $2M$ perturbed attention evaluations per block) and consumed two steps later ($t-2$), discounted future-query key accumulation ($\gamma_K = 0.85, \eta_K = 0.012, \beta_K = 0.98$), deterministic on-the-fly `(xi_q, xi_k)` regeneration, and `BF16`-approximate inverse-RoPE recovery of `(q_n, k_n)` (`exp48_native50m_6mat_active_qk_results.json`, allocating **`44 GiB` at $M=1$ and `50 GiB` at $M=2$ of pinned host RAM** alongside **`10.08–10.24 GiB` GPU VRAM**) improves over both 4-matrix `exp43` and 5-matrix `exp46`:
     1. **Single-Probe Full 8-Block 6-Matrix ($M=1$, `16` Perturbed SDPA Calls/MB, `native50m_strict_post_install_M1_wte15e3_6mat_decoupled_qk_future_g085_triton`):** Achieves **`1.8883 / 1.8877 / 1.8891` $\to$ `1.8884 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.8753 ± 0.0006` VAL-B BPB, `202.96` PPL)** across Seeds `46, 47, 48` in a 3-seed mean of **`5,116.3 ms/step` (`2,081.1 ms` fwd + `3,035.3 ms` rule)**, **`10.08 GiB` peak VRAM**, and `44 GiB` pinned host RAM—improving upon 5-matrix `exp46` (`1.8897 ± 0.0008`, `6,012.9 ms/step`) by **`-0.0013` BPB (`-0.76` PPL)**.
     2. **Amortized Multi-Probe Full 8-Block 6-Matrix ($M=2$, `32` Perturbed SDPA Calls/MB, `native50m_strict_post_install_M1_wte15e3_6mat_decoupled_qk_future_g085_m2_triton`):** Averaging $M=2$ independent local query/key Rademacher probes inside $\bar{u}_q, \bar{u}_k$ on the same cached clean forward activations prior to the single **Stage-1** `bmm` and single `Pre-Polar Muon` step achieves **`1.8851 / 1.8841 / 1.8866` $\to$ `1.8853 ± 0.0013` DEV BPB (`1.8722 ± 0.0012` VAL-B BPB, `201.21` PPL)** across Seeds `46, 47, 48` in a 3-seed mean of **`5,806.4 ms/step` (`2,424.5 ms` fwd + `3,381.9 ms` rule)**, **`10.24 GiB` peak VRAM**, and `50 GiB` pinned host RAM. At the fixed 100-step ($13.1\text{M}$-token) budget, this represents a fixed-token accuracy gain of **`-0.0125` BPB (`-7.18` PPL)** over GPU-resident 4-matrix `exp43` (`1.8978 ± 0.0007`, `3,263.7 ms/step`, `20.65 GiB` VRAM—noting that `exp43` is `1.57–1.78x` faster per step because it keeps traces in `20.65 GiB` GPU memory and leaves $W_Q, W_K$ frozen, so `exp46`, `exp48`, `exp55`, and `exp61` establish fixed-token accuracy and GPU VRAM-footprint gains rather than equal-compute advantages over `exp43`), **`-0.0044` BPB (`-2.51` PPL)** over 5-matrix `exp46` (`1.8897 ± 0.0008`, `6,012.9 ms/step`), and **`-0.0508` BPB (`-30.93` PPL)** in **`1.35x` less step time** over CPU-offloaded 4-matrix `exp32` (`1.9361 ± 0.0004`, `7,857.1 ms/step` `fwd + rule`).
   - **Selective Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ at $M=2$ (`16` SDPA Calls/MB) and $M=4$ (`32` SDPA Calls/MB) under $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`exp55`) and Closed-Form `Wiener-MMSE` + `Hadamard-4` (`exp61`):** Testing whether the upper-half block concentration (`exp50`–`exp56`) and closed-form **Wiener-MMSE Key-Gather filter + Walsh-Hadamard $H_4$ Temporal Multiplexing** (`Proposition 6`, `exp59`–`exp60`) transfer to the 8-layer, $T=2,048$ Native `50,332,176` architecture (`exp55_native50m_upper_half_qk_results.json` and `exp61_native50m_wiener_mmse_key_gather_results.json`, where lower-half blocks `0, 1, 2, 3` keep $W_Q, W_K$ frozen and only upper-half blocks `4, 5, 6, 7`—comprising short-window `S` blocks `4, 5, 6` [$W_l=128$] and full-context `L` block `7` [$W_l=2,048$] in the `SSSLSSSL` layout—receive decoupled $(W_Q, W_K)$ probes, resulting in `48,234,496 / 50,332,176 = 95.83%` active parameters and **`9.96–10.09 GiB` peak GPU VRAM**):
     1. **Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` $M=2$ under $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`16` Perturbed SDPA Calls/MB, `44 GiB` Pinned Host RAM, `native50m_strict_post_install_M1_wte15e3_upper_half_4567_qk_m2_triton`, `exp55`):** Achieves **`1.8876 / 1.8875 / 1.8892` $\to$ `1.8881 ± 0.0010` DEV BPB (`1.8750 ± 0.0010` VAL-B BPB, `202.80` PPL)** in `5,679.7 ms/step` (`2,174.0 ms` fwd + `3,505.7 ms` rule)—retaining **`77.6%` (`-0.0097` of `-0.0125` BPB)** of Full 8-Block $M=2$'s fixed-token gain (`1.8978 -> 1.8853`) at half the SDPA probe budget (`16` vs. `32` calls/mb) and saving `6 GiB` of pinned host RAM (`44` vs. `50 GiB`), while edging out Full 8-Block $M=1$ (`1.8884 ± 0.0007`) at the same `16`-call SDPA budget.
     2. **Fixed-32-SDPA-Probe Reallocation — Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` $M=4$ under $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`32` Perturbed SDPA Calls/MB, `50 GiB` Pinned Host RAM, `native50m_strict_post_install_M1_wte15e3_upper_half_4567_qk_m4_triton`, `exp55`):** Concentrating the exact same `32` perturbed SDPA calls/mb and `50 GiB` pinned host buffer budget of Full 8-Block $M=2$ onto Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` ($M=4$ on blocks `4..7`, $M=0$ on blocks `0..3`) achieves **`1.8849 / 1.8855 / 1.8871` $\to$ `1.8858 ± 0.0011` DEV BPB (`1.8727 ± 0.0012` VAL-B BPB, `201.54` PPL)** in `6,568.7 ms/step` (`2,572.2 ms` fwd + `3,996.5 ms` rule)—retaining **`96.0%` (`-0.0120` of `-0.0125` BPB)** of Full 8-Block $M=2$'s (`1.8853 ± 0.0013`) gain and matching it to within `+0.0005` DEV BPB (`-0.0002, +0.0014, +0.0005` across Seeds `46, 47, 48`) while leaving all lower-half `0, 1, 2, 3` $(W_Q, W_K)$ matrices (`2.10M` parameters) frozen (noting that this is a fixed-token/probe-budget comparison rather than an equal-time reduction, as its `6,568.7 ms/step` exceeds Full 8-Block $M=2$'s `5,806.4 ms/step` due to four-probe PCIe ring-buffer transfers across the upper blocks).
     3. **Closed-Form `Wiener-MMSE` Key Gather on Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` $M=2$ (`16` Perturbed SDPA Calls/MB, `44 GiB` Pinned Host RAM, `native50m_upper_half_4567_wiener_mmse_qk_m2_lrk024_triton`, `exp61` Arm 1):** At $T=2,048$, fixed recency discounting ($\gamma_K=0.85$, effective horizon $\approx 6.7$ tokens) truncates medium- and long-range causal key credit across the 128-token short windows (`4, 5, 6`) and 2,048-token full window (`7`) while summing unattended nearby query noise. Replacing $\gamma_K=0.85$ with the closed-form **Wiener-MMSE Key-Gather filter** $W_{\tau, s}^{\text{MMSE}}(2) = \frac{2 A_{\tau, s}^2}{A_{\tau, s}^2 + \|A_{\tau, :}\|_2^2}$ (`Proposition 6`, reconstructed at consumption time $t$ from cached `BF16` $q_r(t-2), k_r(t-2)$ with `flash_attn` causal window masks and zero extra host-device ring-buffer traffic, $\eta_K=0.024$) improves Upper-Half `(4..7)` $M=2$ across all 3 seeds from `1.8881 ± 0.0010` to **`1.8846 / 1.8851 / 1.8849` $\to$ `1.8849 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB (`1.8717 ± 0.0005` VAL-B BPB, `200.99` PPL)** in **`5,559.1 ms/step` (`2,081.6 ms` fwd + `3,477.5 ms` rule)** and **`9.96 GiB` peak VRAM**—improving over Upper-Half `(4..7)` $M=2$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`exp55`) by **`-0.0032` DEV BPB (`-0.0033` VAL-B, `-1.81` PPL)** and **surpassing even Full 8-Block $M=2$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`1.8853 ± 0.0013` in `5,806.4 ms/step`, `exp48`) at half the SDPA probes (`16` vs. `32` calls/mb) and in `247.3 ms/step` less time!**
     4. **Closed-Form `Wiener-MMSE` + Walsh-Hadamard $H_4$ Temporal Multiplexing on Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` $M=4$ (`32` Perturbed SDPA Calls/MB, `50 GiB` Pinned Host RAM, `native50m_upper_half_4567_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_qk_m4_lrk024_triton`, `exp61` Arm 2):** Combining `Wiener-MMSE` ($W_{\tau, s}^{\text{MMSE}}(4) = \frac{4 A_{\tau, s}^2}{3 A_{\tau, s}^2 + \|A_{\tau, :}\|_2^2}$) with 4-probe Walsh-Hadamard $H_4$ temporal multiplexing (`c(s, i) = (i + (i // 64) + s) mod 4` at $d_h=128$, $\eta_K=0.024$) on Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` $M=4$ achieves **`1.8801 / 1.8797 / 1.8811` $\to$ `1.8803 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.8668 ± 0.0008` VAL-B BPB, `198.42` PPL)** in **`6,293.7 ms/step` (`2,438.9 ms` fwd + `3,854.8 ms` rule)** and **`10.06 GiB` peak VRAM**—breaking both the **`200` DEV PPL barrier (`198.42` PPL)** and **`1.87` VAL-B BPB barrier (`1.8668` VAL-B)** backward-free on Native `50.3M`, improving over Upper-Half `(4..7)` $M=4$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`1.8858 ± 0.0011`, `exp55`) by **`-0.0055` DEV BPB (`-0.0059` VAL-B, `-3.12` PPL)** across all 3 seeds (`-0.0048, -0.0058, -0.0060`), improving over Full 8-Block $M=2$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`1.8853 ± 0.0013`, `exp48`) by **`-0.0050` DEV BPB (`-0.0054` VAL-B, `-2.79` PPL)** at the exact same 32-SDPA-probe budget, and **widening the fixed-token gain over 4-matrix `exp43` (`1.8978 ± 0.0007`) by $1.40\times$ from `-0.0125` to `-0.0175` DEV BPB (`-0.0176` VAL-B, `-9.97` PPL)** (while `-0.0558` DEV BPB / `-33.72` PPL ahead of CPU-offloaded `exp32` `1.9361 ± 0.0004` in `1.25x` less step time).

---

## 5. Limitations and Scope

1. **Short Developmental Horizon, Microbatch Accumulation, Measured Training-Region Timing, Fixed-Token vs. Matched-Time Trade-offs, and Gap to Full Backpropagation:** Our experiments use $0.737\text{M}$–$2.056\text{M}$ tokens on `d6` ($360$–$1,004$ steps across $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$) and $13.1\text{M}$ tokens on Native `50.3M` ($100$ steps), $4 \times 16$ microbatch accumulation on Native `50.3M`, and repeatedly inspected `DEV`/`VAL-B` validation splits (neither validation split provides an untouched confirmation of the selected upper-half or `wiener_mmse` recipes, and the upper-half efficiency advantage is specific to this trainer, probe allocation, and short horizon). All `Run GPU` times in Tables 1–7 and Figure 1E measure CUDA event/synchronized intervals across the forward pass and learning-rule update (`total_train_gpu_s_mean`), excluding data loading, host-to-device batch transfer, and validation passes; moreover, on `d6`, the specialized 4-matrix `exp37`/`exp39` trainer runs at `~18.8 ms/step` (`6.73s` at 360 steps, `13.52s` at 720 steps), whereas the general 6-matrix-capable `exp49`–`exp60` trainer runs at `~21.6 ms/step` for 4-matrix (`15.62s` at 720 steps, `21.55s` at 1,004 steps). On Native `50.3M`, the GPU-resident 4-matrix `exp43` trainer trades `9.11 -> 20.57–20.65 GiB` peak VRAM to eliminate host-device trace transfers (`2.99–3.27 s/step` `fwd + rule`), whereas full pinned host ring buffers (`exp44`, `exp46`, `exp48`, `exp55`, `exp61`) retain **`9.61–10.24 GiB` peak GPU VRAM** while allocating up to **`44–50 GiB` of pinned host RAM** and incurring higher per-step latency (`4.87–6.57 s/step` 3-seed mean `fwd + rule`); consequently, the improvements of 5-matrix `exp46` (`1.8897 ± 0.0008`), Upper-Half `(4..7)` `exp55` (`1.8881 ± 0.0010` at $M=2$ in `5.68 s/step`; `1.8858 ± 0.0011` at $M=4$ in `6.57 s/step`, which is a fixed-token/probe-budget comparison whose step time exceeds Full 8-Block $M=2$'s `5.81 s/step`), 6-matrix `exp48` (`1.8884 ± 0.0007` at $M=1$, `1.8853 ± 0.0013` at $M=2$), and Upper-Half `(4..7)` `Wiener-MMSE` `exp61` (`1.8849 ± 0.0003` at $M=2$ in `5.56 s/step`, which beats Full 8-Block $M=2$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ in both accuracy and step time at half the SDPA probes; and `1.8803 ± 0.0007` [`1.8668` VAL-B, `198.42` PPL] at $M=4$ `Wiener-MMSE + Hadamard-4` in `6.29 s/step`, which is a fixed-token/32-probe comparison) over 4-matrix `exp43` (`1.8978 ± 0.0007`) are fixed-token (`100`-step) accuracy and GPU VRAM-footprint gains rather than equal-wall-clock compute advantages. On `d6`, inside the same `exp49`–`exp60` trainer, uniform full-depth 6-matrix Decoupled Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ $M=2$ is **unfavorable at equal training-region time** against extra-step 4-matrix WM because lower-block `(0, 1, 2)` SDPA probes add $+16.6\%$ step time for only $-0.0013$ to $-0.0023$ BPB conditional gain: at the `~9.35s` budget, 438-step 4-matrix (`2.0454 ± 0.0008` in `9.39s`) beats 360-step Full 6-matrix $M=2$ (`2.0595 ± 0.0007` in `9.35s`), and at the `~21.6s` budget, 1,004-step 4-matrix (`1.9720 ± 0.0006` in `21.55s`) beats 720-step Full 6-matrix $M=2$ (`1.9763 ± 0.0009` in `21.67s` / `1.9756 ± 0.0017` in `21.77s`). However, restricting $(W_Q, W_K)$ probes to the **Upper-Half blocks `(3, 4, 5)`** (`exp50`–`exp59`) captures `90.1%`–`94.4%` of the 720-step full-depth gain (`1.9779 ± 0.0006` in `18.59s` main-RNG-matched; `1.9769 ± 0.0021` in `16.09s` layer-RNG-synced), captures `85.6%`–`92.3%` of the 360-step Full 6-Matrix $M=4$ gain across all four widths $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$ at half the SDPA probes (`2.0814, 2.0562 [2.0540 Wiener-MMSE], 2.0467, 2.0454` vs. `2.0794, 2.0552 [2.0519 Wiener-MMSE + Hadamard-4], 2.0451, 2.0437`), matches 438-step 4-matrix at 400 steps (`2.0454 ± 0.0012` DEV, `2.0085 ± 0.0015` VAL-B vs. `2.0092 ± 0.0006`), **beats both uniform 720-step Full 6-matrix $M=2$ (`1.9756`/`1.9763`) and 1,004-step 4-matrix (`1.9720` in `21.55s`) when all 24 SDPA probes/step are concentrated on Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $M=4$ at 720 steps (`1.9699 ± 0.0001` DEV, `1.9349 ± 0.0001` VAL-B in `21.05s` [`g085`], and `1.9672 ± 0.0017` DEV, `1.9322 ± 0.0017` VAL-B in `22.36s` [`wiener_mmse`]) or 740 steps (`1.9670 ± 0.0002` DEV, `1.9321 ± 0.0002` VAL-B in `19.19s`), and beats 1,004-step 4-matrix by `-0.0111` DEV BPB (`-0.0128` VAL-B BPB, `-6.70` PPL) at matched `~21.6s` training-region time (`840` steps of Upper-Half $M=2$ in `21.71s`: `1.9609 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB, `1.9264 ± 0.0007` VAL-B BPB, `218.03` PPL)**. While 360-step Full 6-Matrix $M=4$ reaches numerical proximity (`+0.0004` to `+0.0020` DEV BPB at $\gamma_K=0.85$ across $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$, and `-0.0011` to `-0.0013` DEV BPB under `wiener_mmse` / `wiener_mmse + hadamard4` at $D=384$: `2.0521 ± 0.0009` and `2.0519 ± 0.0009` vs. `2.0532 ± 0.0008`, rather than statistical equivalence) to the smaller 4-matrix Exact Backpropagation reference (which leaves $W_Q, W_K$ frozen), all backward-free arms still trail matched-subset full 6-matrix `Muon+AdamW` backpropagation (`2.0590, 2.0316, 2.0191, 2.0153` across $D \in \{256..768\}$ at 360 steps and `1.8904 ± 0.0012` [shared `lr=0.006`] / `1.8731 ± 0.0007` [alt weight-row/sphere-proj EMA] / `1.8625 ± 0.0005` [exact `exp49` 6-Mat opt] at 720 steps on `d6`; `1.6989 ± 0.0005` on 100-step Native `50.3M`), and the `d6` diagonal-skip + `lrm -> 0.2, eps = 1e-4` package (`exp40` `1.9845` and `exp41` `2.0252`) does not transfer directly to 100-step Native `50.3M`. Furthermore, our perturbation comparators (`SR-LNP` and `SR-NP`) evaluate author-defined recipes on the 4-matrix subset rather than QLabs' closed-source Dust training pipeline [9].
2. **Perturbation-Differential Plasticity, $\operatorname{diag}(1-x_i^2/D)$ Coordinate Scaling, Stage-2 Leverage Inversion, and Surrogate Comparisons (`exp36`, `exp57`, `exp58`, `exp60`):** Our reward contraction evaluates a stream-partitioned perturbation-differential plasticity tensor $\Delta \tilde{W}^{(m+1, g)}$ against live receiver weights $W^{(m+1)}(t_{\text{constr}})$, and Proposition 3 uses the diagonal skip $W_{\text{skip}} = \alpha_0 I_D$ as an algebraic surrogate. As shown in Proposition 1(c), `exp58`, and `exp60`, coordinatewise WM followed by radial projection has first-order conditional mean $\mathbb{E}[P_x\hat{\mu}_{\text{WM}}^{(1)}] = \frac{1}{\text{rms}(z)}P_x\operatorname{diag}(1 - x_i^2/D)a \neq \frac{1}{\text{rms}(z)}P_x a = \mu_{\text{skip}}$ (with modulation-vector discrepancy bounded by $\left(\frac{\max_i x_i^2}{D}\right)\frac{\|a\|_2}{\text{rms}(z)}$ and outer-product matrix Frobenius discrepancy bounded by $\left(\frac{\max_i x_i^2}{D}\right)\frac{\|a\|_2 \|h_n\|_2}{\text{rms}(z)}$, where the normalized discrepancy coefficient $\max_i x_i^2/D$ is $O(1/D)$ only under coordinate concentration $\max_i x_i^2 = O(1)$, whereas applying the closed-form Stage-2 leverage inversion $\operatorname{diag}(1-x_i^2/D)^{-1}$ prior to $P_x$ restores exact equality $\cos = 1.000000$ at $D=3$ and scores `2.0519 ± 0.0009` at 360st and `1.9632 ± 0.0009` at 720st in `exp60` Arms 3 & 6), and as shown in `exp57`–`exp58` (cf. [5]), Sub-Block Direct-Skip Surrogate BP (`1.9819` on 4-Mat; `1.9367` shared-LR / `1.9016` exact `exp49` 6-Mat opt / `1.9128` alt weight-proj EMA on 6-Mat at 720 steps) omits both inter-block composition and intra-block `attn -> mlp` credit (`+0.0006`, `+0.0033`, and `+0.0047` BPB relative to Block-Skip BP `1.9813`, `1.9334`, and `1.8969`). Moreover, as shown in `exp36` Part B, folding internal `Pre-Polar Muon` (`2.1613` vs. `2.1579`) or `lm_head` `AdamW` preconditioning (`2.2128` vs. `2.1579`) into the reader degrades learning because forward perturbations propagate through $W_{\text{live}}$.
3. **Three-Tier Attention Credit Hierarchy, Pure $M=4$ vs. Exploratory `ve_gate`, Closed-Form `Wiener-MMSE` / `Hadamard-4` / `Sparse Top-K=32` Key Gather, and Remaining Subspace Variance:** Unfreezing all 6 principal hidden matrix families (`26,345,472 / 26,346,060 = 99.9978%` active parameters on `d6`, plus the `576` `ve_gate` weights at zero-based blocks `1, 3, 5` for `26,346,048 = 99.99995%` active parameters in `exp49`/`exp51`, where the 360-step isolated `ve_gate` paired difference is $-0.00047 \pm 0.00064$ BPB and 720-step pure $M=4$ without `ve_gate` [`1.9672 ± 0.0005` in `27.71s`, `exp53`] outperforms $M=4+\text{ve\_gate}$ [`1.9683 ± 0.0007` in `28.57s`, `exp51`] by `-0.0011 ± 0.0007` BPB) via decoupled query/key probes, discounted future-query key accumulation ($\gamma_K = 0.85$) or closed-form **Wiener-MMSE / Hadamard-$4$ / Sparse Top-$K=32$ Key Gather** ($W_{\tau, s}^{\text{MMSE}}(M)$, `Proposition 6`, `exp59`–`exp61`), and $M \in \{2, 4\}$ amortized multi-probe averaging (`exp47`–`exp61`) reaches **`2.0794, 2.0552 [2.0521 Wiener-MMSE / 2.0519 Hadamard-4], 2.0451, 2.0437` DEV BPB** across $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$ at 360 steps (`M=4`), **`1.9672 ± 0.0005` DEV BPB (`221.80` PPL, $\gamma_K=0.85$), `1.9632 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`1.9284 ± 0.0006` VAL-B, `219.39` PPL, `wiener_mmse` in `29.14s`), `1.9633 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB (`219.42` PPL, sparse `Top-K=32` in `24.36s`), and `1.9632 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB (`1.9283 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `219.37` PPL, `Hadamard-4 + LevInv` in `24.54s`)** at 720 steps (`Pure 6-Matrix M=4`), **`1.9699 ± 0.0001` DEV BPB (`223.42` PPL in `21.05s`, $\gamma_K=0.85$) and `1.9672 ± 0.0017` DEV BPB (`221.79` PPL in `22.36s`, `wiener_mmse`)** at 720 steps (`Upper-Half (3,4,5) M=4`), **`1.9670 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB (`1.9321 ± 0.0002` VAL-B, `221.69` PPL)** at 740 steps (`Upper-Half (3,4,5) M=4` in `19.19s`), **`1.9609 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`1.9264 ± 0.0007` VAL-B, `218.03` PPL)** at 840 steps (`Upper-Half (3,4,5) M=2` in `21.71s`), and **`1.8858 ± 0.0011` DEV BPB (`201.54` PPL, Upper-Half `(4..7)` $M=4$ $\gamma_K=0.85$), `1.8853 ± 0.0013` DEV BPB (`201.21` PPL, Full 6-Matrix $M=2$ $\gamma_K=0.85$), `1.8849 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB (`1.8717 ± 0.0005` VAL-B, `200.99` PPL, Upper-Half `(4..7)` $M=2$ `Wiener-MMSE`), and `1.8803 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.8668 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `198.42` PPL, Upper-Half `(4..7)` $M=4$ `Wiener-MMSE + Hadamard-4`)** on Native `50.3M` (`95.83%` and `99.9990%` active parameters). Nevertheless, because Tier-2 query probes share a scalar per-head reward across $d_h$ channels and Tier-3 key probes experience cross-time superposition variance across causal positions $s \le \tau$ (plus shrinkage bias $(1 - W_{\tau, s})A_{\tau, s} c_{\tau, s}$ under `wiener_mmse` or truncation bias for $\gamma_K < 1$, and `BF16` rounding in inverse-RoPE recovery on Native `50.3M`), backward-free $(W_Q, W_K)$ updates capture **`69.8%` ($D=256$), `60.2%`–`66.2%` ($D=384$), `53.4%` ($D=512$), and `43.2%` ($D=768$)** at $M=4$ of the exact-backpropagation 4-to-6-matrix attention-routing gain at 360 steps, and **`43.5%`–`44.9%` at $M=2$ (`-0.0226` / `-0.0233` vs. `-0.0519` BPB), `59.0%` at $M=4 + \text{ve\_gate}$ (`-0.0306` vs. `-0.0519` BPB), `61.1%` at pure $M=4$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`-0.0317` vs. `-0.0519` BPB), and `68.8%` at pure $M=4$ `wiener_mmse` (`-0.0357` vs. `-0.0519` BPB)** of the exact-backpropagation 4-to-6-matrix gain at 720 steps (`1.9423 -> 1.8904`).

## 6. Reproducibility and Supplement Manifest

The attached `supplement.zip` ($1.99\text{ MB}$ uncompressed, $0.51\text{ MB}$ compressed; strictly under the $2,000,000$-byte limit) contains `figure-1.png`, all self-contained training scripts, retained 3-seed JSON records (preserving full intermediate checkpoint trajectories `step -> {dev_bpb, valb_bpb, dev_ppl}` across `exp59`–`exp61` and 3-point start/midpoint/end checkpoint trajectories across `exp53`–`exp58`, whose full 60-step intermediate series are permanently archived in `/problems/general_pretraining/papers/20/v9`, while full intermediate checkpoint series for `exp26`–`exp51` remain available in `/problems/general_pretraining/papers/20/v8`), uncontended timing and isolated kernel-substitution benchmarks, geometric audit code, figure generation code, and a pure-Python verification script:
- `verify_paper5.py`: Runs in `< 0.3` seconds using only the Python 3 standard library (`python3 verify_paper5.py`). It verifies **Propositions 1–6** (`Checks 1–4`, including exhaustive $2^N$ and $2^D$ enumerations, Proposition 1c's $D=3$ 8-pattern enumeration verifying $\mathbb{E}[P_x\hat{\mu}_{\text{WM}}^{(1)}] = (1/4, -1/4, 1)^\top \neq \mu_{\text{skip}} = (1/2, -1/2, 1)^\top$, closed-form Stage-2 leverage inversion $\operatorname{diag}(1-x_i^2/D)^{-1}$ restoring exact equality $\cos = 1.000000$, and outer-product Frobenius discrepancy bound $\left(\frac{\max_i x_i^2}{D}\right)\frac{\|a_n\|_2 \|h_n\|_2}{\text{rms}(z_n)}$, causal attention commutation, diagonal skip identity, sparse vs. bounded-kurtosis `RMSNorm` expectation bounds, Proposition 5's unprojected vs. tangent-projected reward-reconstruction variance identity, and Proposition 6's closed-form Wiener-MMSE key-gather optimality, 3-token Rademacher enumeration, Walsh-Hadamard $H_4$ probe multiplexing orthogonality, and Sparse Top-$K=32$ energy retention), verifies the **2-token finite-difference key truncation vs. future-query accumulation illustration**, executes a **deterministic pure-Python twin optimizer unit test + source-correspondence check** verifying that the `exact_exp49_opt=True` update loop in `run_exp58_block_vs_subblock_skip_and_recipe_matched_controls.py`, `run_exp59_rake_key_gather_and_clock_controls.py`, `run_exp60_hadamard_qk_multiplexing_and_leverage_inversion.py`, and `run_exp61_native50m_wiener_mmse_key_gather.py` matches `run_exp49_d6_equal_compute_and_ve_gate.py` (`lines 547–560`: Nesterov lookahead $v_t = (1-\beta)g_t + \beta b_t$, 5-step `PolarExpress` orthogonalization, decoupled weight decay `0.01`, and Frobenius norm restoration on $W_Q, W_K$) whereas `wm_recipe_matched=True` executes the distinct weight-row/sphere-projected non-Nesterov EMA update, and audits all **`208` multi-seed experimental arms (`623` total runs: `145` three-seed strictly delayed zero-relay Delayed-WM arms, `13` three-seed node-perturbation arms, `4` head/lexical-only backward-free anchors, `3` three-seed same-clock compositional-relay controls, `7` three-seed local-pullback controls, and `36` three-seed backpropagation references [`21` global BP + `5` block-skip surrogate BP + `10` sub-block direct-skip surrogate BP])** across `exp18_native50m_results.json`, `exp21_results.json`, `exp26_results.json`–`exp51_d6_720step_scaling_and_bp_controls_results.json`, `exp53_d6_compositional_and_rng_controls_results.json`, `exp54_d6_indep_comp_and_bus_multiprobe_results.json`,
